# External-sector scorecard, every country 1995-2024 (World Bank)

Escrow esc_379d14f823434fa18ba47212, job job_8d4d75dfb9aa4068. All scorecard figures, rankings and lists below are computed from **macro-external-pull3.csv** (the last good pull; pull 2's three indicator requests failed after one retry, see PROOF). Periods 1995-2004, 2005-2014, 2015-2024; mean over years with a value, n = years used, n/a = no value in the period; change = last-period mean minus first-period mean (n/a if either is n/a). Units: percent (growth) / percent of GDP (shares). Figures rounded to 2 decimals, half away from zero, from unrounded values. Aggregates (region id NA) excluded.

## Changes: PULL 2 vs PULL 1

Matched by key countryiso3code|date, macro-external-pull1.csv (earlier) vs macro-external-pull2.csv (later); compared columns: export growth, investment share, current account.
Rows: PULL 1 = 6510, PULL 2 = 6510 (6510 = 6510 + 0 - 0). new = 0, gone = 0, changed = 14854 cells in 5859 keys.

PULL 2 is not a failed pull: its country-list request succeeded, so it keeps every country-year row; its three indicator requests timed out after one retry (PROOF, PULL 2 requests), so those values are empty and count as done per the TASK. It is compared by key like any pull.

new: none

gone: none

changed: every changed cell, listed by key. Format: `key|column:earlier>later`, several cells of the same key on one line separated by `|`. Column codes: X = export growth, I = investment share, C = current account. Nothing after `>` (or before it) means the cell is empty (null) in that CSV. Example: `ABW|1995|I:31.1336717428088>` = key ABW|1995, column investment share, earlier 31.1336717428088, later empty.

## Changes: PULL 3 vs PULL 2

Matched by key countryiso3code|date, macro-external-pull2.csv (earlier) vs macro-external-pull3.csv (later); compared columns: export growth, investment share, current account.
Rows: PULL 2 = 6510, PULL 3 = 6510 (6510 = 6510 + 0 - 0). new = 0, gone = 0, changed = 14854 cells in 5859 keys.

PULL 3's indicator requests all succeeded; macro-external-pull3.csv is byte-identical to macro-external-pull1.csv (same SHA-256), so each change below restores the PULL 1 value.

new: none

gone: none

changed: every changed cell, listed by key. Format: `key|column:earlier>later`, several cells of the same key on one line separated by `|`. Column codes: X = export growth, I = investment share, C = current account. Nothing after `>` (or before it) means the cell is empty (null) in that CSV. Example: `ABW|1995|I:31.1336717428088>` = key ABW|1995, column investment share, earlier 31.1336717428088, later empty.
